```css
:root {
  --bg: #e9e9e7;
  --fg: #1b1b1b;
  --muted: #4a4a48;
  --surface: #f4f4f2;
  --surface-2: #dcdcd9;
  --border: #c9c9c5;
  --accent: #e85d04;
  --accent-fg: #111111;
  --accent-2: #1b1b1b;
  --hero-fg: #ffffff;
  --hero-overlay: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(27, 27, 27, 0.35), rgba(27, 27, 27, 0.7));
  --radius: 0px;
  --radius-lg: 0px;
  --shadow: none;
  --font-heading: "Barlow Condensed", Impact, sans-serif;
  --font-body: "Barlow", system-ui, sans-serif;
  --h1: clamp(3rem, 9vw, 7.5rem);
  --h2: clamp(2.2rem, 5vw, 4rem);
}
h1, h2, h3, .btn, .eyebrow, .hero-eyebrow, .site-nav a, .tagline-big { text-transform: uppercase; }
h1, h2 { font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.01em; line-height: 0.95; }
h3 { font-weight: 700; font-size: 1.5rem; letter-spacing: 0.02em; }
.eyebrow { color: var(--fg); padding-left: 44px; position: relative; }
.eyebrow::before { content: ""; position: absolute; left: 0; top: 50%; width: 32px; height: 10px; transform: translateY(-50%); background: repeating-linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent) 0 6px, var(--fg) 6px 12px); }
.site-header { border-bottom: 4px solid var(--fg); }
.site-header::after { content: ""; display: block; height: 6px; background: repeating-linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent) 0 14px, var(--fg) 14px 28px); }
.btn { border-radius: 0; font-family: var(--font-heading); font-size: 1.05rem; letter-spacing: 0.06em; padding: 14px 28px; }
.btn-primary { background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-fg); border-color: var(--accent); }
.hero-bullets li::before { background: var(--accent); border-radius: 0; }
.num-item .num { color: var(--accent); font-size: 3rem; }
.card, .feature .pillar, .form, .step, .stat { border: 2px solid var(--fg); background: var(--surface); }
.table th, .table td { border-bottom: 2px solid var(--fg); }
.cta-box { background: var(--fg); color: #ffffff; clip-path: polygon(0 0, 100% 6%, 100% 100%, 0 94%); padding-block: 96px; }
.cta-box .btn-primary { background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-fg); border-color: var(--accent); }
.page-hero.has-media { clip-path: polygon(0 0, 100% 0, 100% 92%, 0 100%); }
.site-footer { background: var(--fg); color: #ffffff; border-top: 0; }
.site-footer a, .site-footer .footer-copy { color: #ffffff; }
.site-footer .iso-badge { border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent); }
.site-footer .footer-top { border-bottom-color: #444444; }
.site-footer .btn-primary { background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-fg); }
.word-grid li { border-left: 6px solid var(--accent); border-radius: 0; }
```

```css
.word-grid li { border-left: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--accent-2); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--accent-2); background: transparent; text-align: center; font-weight: 600; }
.site-footer { background: var(--surface); border-top: 3px double var(--accent-2); }
.page-hero { background: var(--surface); text-align: center; }
.page-hero h1 { margin-inline: auto; }
.page-hero .lead { margin-inline: auto; }
.breadcrumb { justify-content: center; }
.faq-item h3 { font-size: 1.4rem; }
```

```css
  --accent-fg: #101014;
  --accent-2: #ffb08a;
  --hero-fg: #ffffff;
  --hero-overlay: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(16, 16, 20, 0.2), rgba(16, 16, 20, 0.85));
  --radius: 12px;
  --radius-lg: 20px;
  --shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  --font-heading: "Space Grotesk", system-ui, sans-serif;
  --font-body: "Space Grotesk", system-ui, sans-serif;
  --h1: clamp(2.8rem, 7.5vw, 6rem);
}
h1, h2 { letter-spacing: -0.03em; }
.num.big { font-size: 5.5rem; color: transparent; -webkit-text-stroke: 2px var(--accent); line-height: 0.9; }
.scroll-card { flex-basis: min(86vw, 440px); padding: 32px; }
.scroll-card .card-media { border-radius: var(--radius); }
.scroll-track { padding-bottom: 32px; }
.scroll-track::-webkit-scrollbar { height: 6px; }
.scroll-track::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--accent); border-radius: 3px; }
.eyebrow { color: var(--accent); }
.hero-eyebrow { color: var(--accent-2); }
.btn-ghost { border-color: rgba(245, 245, 247, 0.5); }
.stat-value { color: var(--accent); }
.card, .stat, .feature .pillar, .form, .step { background: var(--surface); }
.cta-box { background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-fg); }
.cta-box .btn-primary { background: var(--accent-fg); color: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent-fg); }
.site-footer { background: #0b0b0e; }
.nav-transparent.is-scrolled { background: rgba(16, 16, 20, 0.92); color: var(--fg); }
.page-hero { background: var(--surface); }
.form input:not([type="checkbox"]), .form textarea { background: #101014; }
```

```css
.site-footer .logo { color: #ffffff; }
@media (max-width: 1023px) { .feature, .closing, .cta { background-attachment: scroll; } .feature .stats { margin-top: calc(-1 * var(--section-y) - 24px); } }
```

```css
.cta-box { background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), var(--accent-2)); }
.site-footer { background: #ffffff; border-top: 0; }
.page-hero { background: transparent; }
```

```css
  --font-body: "IBM Plex Sans", system-ui, sans-serif;

.eyebrow, .hero-eyebrow, .num, .label, .stat-value, .stat-label, .breadcrumb, .lang-toggle, .footer-iso, .tl-marker, .site-nav a, .btn, .hero-bullets { font-family: "IBM Plex Mono", ui-monospace, monospace; }

.eyebrow::after, .hero-eyebrow::after { content: " ]"; }
.eyebrow, .hero-eyebrow { color: var(--accent); letter-spacing: 0.1em; }

.hero-title::after { content: "_"; color: var(--accent); animation: blink 1s steps(2) infinite; }
@keyframes blink { 50% { opacity: 0; } }
.hero-bullets li::before { background: var(--accent); border-radius: 0; }

.site-nav a { font-size: 0.85rem; letter-spacing: 0.08em; text-transform: uppercase; }

.btn-ghost { border-color: rgba(215, 227, 238, 0.5); }
.num-item { border-color: var(--border); }
.num-item .num::before { content: "> "; }
.card, .stat, .feature .pillar, .form, .step { background: var(--surface); border-color: var(--border); }
.stat { border-left: 3px solid var(--accent); }
.stat-value { color: var(--accent); }
.tl::before { background: var(--border); }
.tl-marker { background: var(--bg); color: var(--accent); border: 1px solid var(--accent); border-radius: 3px; }
.cta-box { background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--accent); color: var(--fg); }
.cta-box .btn-primary { background: var(--accent); color: var(--accent-fg); border-color: var(--accent); }
.site-footer { background: #070a0e; }
.page-hero { background: var(--surface); }
.form input:not([type="checkbox"]), .form textarea { background: #0b0f14; font-family: "IBM Plex Mono", ui-monospace, monospace; }
```

```css
.cta-box { background: #000000; color: #ffffff; border-radius: 0; }
.cta-box .btn-primary { background: var(--accent-2); color: #000000; border-color: var(--accent-2); }
.site-footer { background: #ffffff; border-top: 3px solid #000000; }
.iso-badge { border-color: #000000; color: #000000; }

.demo-badge { color: #000000; }
```

```json
}
```

```css
.statement { font-size: clamp(1.6rem, 3.2vw, 2.6rem); }

.cta-box .btn-primary { background: #ffffff; color: #1f6feb; border-color: #ffffff; }

.page-hero { background: var(--surface); }
.form input:not([type="checkbox"]), .form textarea { background: #0d1117; }
```

```css
:root {
  --bg: #ffffff;
  --fg: #0a0a0a;
  --muted: #555555;
  --surface: #f5f5f5;
  --surface-2: #ebebeb;
  --border: #dcdcdc;
  --accent: #0a0a0a;
  --accent-fg: #ffffff;
  --accent-2: #888888;
  --hero-fg: #ffffff;
  --hero-overlay: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6));
  --radius: 0px;
  --radius-lg: 0px;
  --shadow: none;
  --font-heading: "Libre Caslon Text", Georgia, serif;
  --font-body: "Work Sans", system-ui, sans-serif;
  --max-width: 1040px;
  --section-y: 140px;
  --h1: clamp(2.8rem, 7vw, 6rem);
  --lh: 1.75;
}
img, .hero-video, .hero-media { filter: grayscale(1) contrast(1.05); }
h1, h2, h3 { font-weight: 400; letter-spacing: -0.01em; }
.hero-title { font-style: italic; }
.eyebrow, .hero-eyebrow, .label, .breadcrumb, .site-nav a, .lang-toggle, .btn { font-family: var(--font-body); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.2em; font-size: 0.72rem; }
.eyebrow { color: var(--muted); }
.section { border-top: 1px solid var(--border); }
.row { border-top: 0; padding-block: 48px; }
.row-body .num { color: var(--muted); }
.card, .feature .pillar, .form, .step { background: transparent; border: 1px solid var(--border); }
.btn { border-radius: 0; padding: 16px 32px; }
.btn-primary { background: var(--fg); color: #ffffff; }
.hero-bullets li::before { background: #ffffff; }
.cta-box { background: transparent; color: var(--fg); border-top: 1px solid var(--fg); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--fg); }
.cta-box .btn-primary { background: var(--fg); color: #ffffff; border-color: var(--fg); }
.word-grid li { background: transparent; border-left: 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--fg); font-weight: 400; font-style: italic; }
.site-footer { background: #ffffff; }
.page-hero { background: #ffffff; }
.page-hero.has-media { color: #ffffff; }
.nav-overlay a { font-family: var(--font-heading); font-style: italic; text-transform: none; letter-spacing: 0; }
.nav-hamburger.is-scrolled, body.nav-hamburger-layout:not(.page-home) .nav-hamburger:not(.is-scrolled) { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); }
.masonry .card { padding: 32px; }
```

```css
:root {
  --bg: #ffffff;
  --fg: #1c1c28;
  --muted: #5c5c6e;
  --surface: #f6f6fa;
  --surface-2: #ececf4;
  --border: #e0e0ea;
  --accent: #6d28d9;
  --accent-fg: #ffffff;
  --accent-2: #c4b5fd;
  --hero-fg: #ffffff;

.eyebrow { color: var(--accent); }
.hero-bullets li::before { background: var(--accent-2); }

.tl-item { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 48px 1fr; gap: 24px; padding: 0 0 56px; }
.tl-marker { position: sticky; top: 100px; align-self: start; width: 48px; height: 48px; font-size: 1rem; box-shadow: 0 0 0 6px var(--bg); }

.btn { border-radius: 999px; }
.cta-box { background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), #4c1d95); }

.page-hero { background: var(--surface); }
```

## Self-review notes (completed while writing)

- Spec coverage: §4.1 layout → Tasks 1–22; §4.2 content model → Tasks 3–4; §4.3 variants → Tasks 8–14; §4.4 recipe → Task 7; §4.5 build → Task 19; §4.6 URLs → Task 2 + Task 19 relative links; §5 demos → Tasks 17, 23–33; §6 video → Tasks 9, 15, 16, 18 (posters); §7 toggle → Tasks 8, 14; §8 navigation/badge → Tasks 8, 14, 16; §9 forms/legal → Tasks 12, 13, 16; §10 accessibility/contrast → Tasks 15, 20; §11 deploy → Task 22; §12 verification → Tasks 20, 21, 34; §13 build errors → Tasks 3, 7, 19; §14 phases → Tasks 1–22 (phase 1), 23–28 (phase 2), 29–34 (phase 3); §15 out of scope → honoured.
- Type consistency: `ctx` fields (`lang, t, demo, page, paths, video, demoIndex, demoCount, logo`) are identical in `tests/support/ctx.js` (Task 8) and `build/build.js` (Task 19). Variant function signatures: `(ctx)` everywhere except `faq.*(ctx, data)`; `careers(ctx, faqVariant)` passes `C.faq`. `checkDist({ out, root })` returns `{ errors, files }` and both Task 20 tests and the CLI use that shape.
